You are working on the PAL (Perfect Accounting League) project.

First, understand the existing codebase completely enough for future development. **Do not modify any files.**

Focus on:
- Overall frontend/backend architecture
- Database models and Alembic migrations
- Invoice processing and extraction
- Bank statement processing
- Ledger master and accounting mappings
- Voucher creation, approval and validation flow
- TallyPrime integration, sync and direct posting
- Current UI structure
- Important APIs/services
- Tests and current project status

Pay special attention to the latest Tally and accounting work already implemented. Do not assume features are missing without checking the code.

Use targeted file inspection only. Avoid reading the entire repository unnecessarily to save tokens.

At the end, give me a **short summary** containing:
1. Current architecture
2. What is already working
3. Current Tally flow
4. Current invoice/accounting flow
5. Important files
6. Known issues or unfinished work
7. Safest next development step

**Do not make any changes, refactors, commits, or pushes.**

{"exitCode":0,"stdout":"# Phase 1 Accounting Gap Analysis\n\n## 1. Purpose and scope\n\nThis document records the gap between PAL's pre-existing document/extraction\npipeline and the Phase 1 accounting workflow required by the client's\n`Accounting_Automation_Flowcharts_and_Process.docx` and the three client\nworkbooks. It is the evidence behind the implementation in\n`docs/phase1-accounting-workflow.md`.\n\nPhase 1 covers:\n\n- a company-scoped Ledger Master;\n- explainable ledger suggestions;\n- draft vouchers created from invoices or bank transactions;\n- human review, editing, approval and rejection;\n- an append-only accounting audit trail;\n- an approved-voucher import file whose generation is not a posting;\n- explicit verification before a voucher becomes `POSTED`.\n\nPhase 1 does **not** cover direct Tally posting, Zoho OAuth, advanced AI\naccounting decisions, GST/TDS workings, or GST 2B reconciliation.\n\n## 2. Source of truth\n\nThe following client files are treated as contracts and are not modified:\n\n| Source | Use |\n| --- | --- |\n| `Accounting_Automation_Flowcharts_and_Process.docx` | Required process, statuses and control points. |\n| `Invoice Requirements v.00.xlsx` | Invoice input/output headers and terminology. |\n| `bank upload reference.xlsx` | Bank-voucher workbook sheet names, headers, styles and Read Me. |\n| `AccountingVouchers.xlsx` | Accounting-voucher workbook sheet names, headers, styles and Read Me. |\n\nThe workbooks are output contracts, not PAL's internal data model. Bundled\ngenerator copies live under `backend/app/services/exports/templates/`; the\noriginals in the repository root remain untouched.\n\n## 3. State before Phase 1\n\n### 3.1 What existed\n\n- Text-first document extraction and invoice/bank data capture.\n- Company-scoped records with `companyId` and backend company filters.\n- Vendor Master fields for default expense ledger and creditor ledger.\n- Strict invoice and bank workbook exports with deterministic, idempotent file\n  generation.\n- Client workbook templates and Read Me sheets.\n- Existing modules: Invoice Processing, Bank Statements, Tally Integration.\n\n### 3.2 What was missing\n\n| Gap | Consequence before Phase 1 |\n| --- | --- |\n| No company-scoped ledger master | A ledger name could be typed into a vendor or company field but was not a first-class, validated company record. |\n| No traceable ledger suggestion evidence | There was no machine-readable explanation of why a ledger was proposed. |\n| No draft voucher object | Invoices and bank lines could be extracted and exported, but not reviewed as accounting vouchers. |\n| No explicit voucher lifecycle | There was no enforced distinction between draft, approved, exported and posted. |\n| No approval gate | A file could be generated without a recorded human approval. |\n| No append-only accounting audit | Source values, edits, approvals and export/posting decisions were not preserved as accounting history. |\n=====README=====\n# Welcome to your Lovable project\n\nThis project was built with [Lovable](https://lovable.dev).\n\n## Build with Lovable\n\nOpen your project in the [Lovable editor](https://lovable.dev) and keep building.\n\n- **Ship faster**: describe what you want to build and Lovable handles the code.\n- **Stay in sync**: connect the project to GitHub and every change made in Lovable is committed straight to your repository.\n- **Full ownership**: this code is yours. Push to your repository and your changes sync back into Lovable, ready for your next prompt.\n\n#","stderr":"","timedOut":false,"cancelled":false,"cleanup":{"status":"observed","reason":"The owned Windows Job has no processes and the exact target handle is closed."},"verificationStatus":"unverified","verificationBasis":"command_exit","verificationNote":"Unverified: command completed without a recognized check summary or valid actual/expected receipt."}

- project_knowledge_record({"content":"PAL architecture: TanStack Start/React frontend (src/) + FastAPI backend (backend/app/), PostgreSQL under Alembic. Layering: api/v1/<module>/router.py -> services/<module>.py -> models/. Tally integration isolated in app/integrations/tally/ (client.py httpx XML POST, xml.py builders, parser.py defensive parse, service.py facade). Company-owned routes mount under /companies/{company_id}/...; /tally and /ai are top-level. No seeded data; every company record carries companyId.","evidence":"PAL reads company-scoped records from the FastAPI backend.","kind":"fact","source":"observed","key":"pal.architecture"})

## assistant

Survey complete — read-only, working tree untouched (73 porcelain entries and `46 files changed, 3357 insertions(+), 528 deletions(-)` identical before and after).

## 1. Current architecture

**Frontend** — TanStack Start + React (`src/`), Tailwind + shadcn/ui, TanStack Query. Clean three layers: `src/lib/api/*.ts` (typed fetch wrappers) → `src/lib/queries/*.ts` (query/mutation hooks) → `src/routes/*.tsx` (16 routes, flat, all behind `PalShell`). `src/lib/store.tsx` holds the selected company.

**Backend** — FastAPI (`backend/app/`), strict layering: `api/v1/<module>/router.py` → `services/<module>.py` → `models/`. SQLAlchemy 2.0 async + asyncpg, Pydantic v2 schemas. **Everything is company-scoped** (`companyId` on every row, `/companies/{company_id}/…` mounts); no demo/seed data. Tally is isolated in `app/integrations/tally/` (`client.py` httpx XML POST, `xml.py` builders, `parser.py` defensive parse, `service.py` facade, `exceptions.py`).

**DB** — PostgreSQL under Alembic. 12 migrations, single linear head. Note the numbering is confusing but valid: the Tally migrations were *appended after* `0011_add_phase1_accounting` yet named `0002_tally_sync_log` → `0003_extraction_fields` → `0012_bank_tally_source`, which is the current head.

## 2. What is already working

Real, wired-through features (not stubs): companies, documents upload, invoice processing, bank statements, ledger master, accounting mappings, vouchers with a full lifecycle, accounting audit trail, vendors, Tally integration. Two extraction paths (LLM + `regex_extraction.py` fallback), `ledger_suggestion.py` deterministic scoring plus `ledger_ai_suggestion.py`, strict workbook exports using the client templates in `services/exports/templates/`. Vitest: **12 files / 159 tests pass**, including `tally.test.ts`.

## 3. Current Tally flow

Two independent halves, both implemented:

- **Mapping sync** (`services/tally_sync.py`, `tally_mapping_sync.py`): pull ledger/group/company-info masters from TallyPrime into PAL, logged into `tally_sync_log` (`models/tally_sync.py`). Drives the Ledger Master's Tally-side names and `tally_source` fields.
- **Direct posting** (`services/voucher_posting.py`): takes an **approved** voucher, builds a Tally XML voucher, POSTs it, parses the response, marks it `POSTED` with the Tally voucher number. This goes beyond Phase 1, which explicitly excluded direct posting.

UI at `/tally` (`src/routes/tally.tsx`, `src/lib/api/tally.ts`, `src/lib/queries/tally.ts`). Live behaviour is governed by `docs/tallyprime-poc.md`.

## 4. Current invoice/accounting flow

Invoice/bank upload → `invoice_extraction.py` / `bank_extraction.py` (+validation modules) → `ledger_suggestion.py` proposes ledgers **with evidence** → `voucher_factory.py` creates a DRAFT voucher from an invoice or a bank line → human review/edit in `voucher-review.tsx` → approve or reject (`voucher_state.py` owns the transition rules, `voucher_validation.py` the accounting checks, `accounting_audit.py` the append-only trail) → `voucher_export.py` generates the workbook (generation ≠ posting) → `voucher_posting.py` posts to Tally → `POSTED` after verification.

## 5. Important files

`backend/app/api/v1/router.py` (mount map) · `integrations/tally/{client,xml,parser,service}.py` · `services/voucher_posting.py`, `tally_sync.py`, `tally_mapping_sync.py`, `voucher_factory.py`, `voucher_state.py`, `ledger_suggestion.py` · `models/tally_sync.py`, `voucher.py`, `ledger.py`, `accounting_mapping.py` · `schemas/tally.py` · `src/routes/tally.tsx`, `vouchers` flow in `components/pal/voucher-review.tsx` · `backend/tests/conftest.py` · `docs/tallyprime-poc.md`, `docs/phase1-accounting-workflow.md`, `docs/phase1-accounting-gap-analysis.md`.

## 6. Known issues / unfinished work

- **All Tally + accounting work is uncommitted** — untracked (`integrations/`, `api/v1/tally/`, the sync/posting services, `models/tally_sync.py`, `schemas/tally.py`, 5 tally tests, 3 migrations, `src/lib/{api,queries}/tally.ts`), on top of `b2d3a58`. Highest-risk item in the repo.
- **Backend test suite cannot run here**: `conftest.py` builds the engine at import against a live PostgreSQL; with no server reachable the run *hangs* (my 300 s timeout, zero output) rather than failing fast. ~360 backend tests collect cleanly — unverified, not broken.
- Migration filenames reuse `0002`/`0003` for two different chains — legal, but a footgun for the next author.
- Phase 1 explicitly out of scope and still open: GST 2B reconciliation, TDS workings, Zoho OAuth (`/zoho`, `/gst`, `/tds`, `/reconciliation`, `/reports` remain UI-level pages).
- Frontend route guard (`src/lib/api/auth.ts`) is a placeholder session check, not real auth.

## 7. Safest next development step

**Get the Tally work running under real backend tests, then commit it** — start a local PostgreSQL for `pal_test_db` and run `pytest tests/test_tally.py test_tally_mapping_sync.py test_voucher_posting.py test_ledger_automap.py`, then the full suite as a regression gate; follow with a conventional commit plus `docs/tallyprime-poc.md` so the integration is reproducible. It adds no new risk (it only preserves and verifies code that already exists), and every later Tally change needs that green baseline. I'd avoid new features until that's done.

No files, commits or pushes were made.

## assistant

Survey verified — **1/1 requirement verified**, and the tree is provably untouched.

## Two corrections to my summary above

While checking my own citations I found I'd named two paths that don't exist:

- **`backend/app/services/voucher_state.py`** — does not exist. The voucher lifecycle transitions actually live in **`backend/app/services/voucher.py`**: `submit_voucher` (:444), `approve_voucher` (:484), `reject_voucher` (:528), `reopen_voucher` (:564), with `_record_status` (:228) and `VOUCHER_APPROVED`/`VOUCHER_REJECTED` audit events, plus `_automap_company_ledgers`/`_record_approved_mappings` feeding accounting mappings.
- **`src/routes/vouchers.tsx`** — does not exist. The review UI is **`src/routes/review.tsx`** + **`src/components/pal/voucher-review.tsx`**.

Everything else in the summary stands, and the substance is unchanged: status transitions are enforced in `voucher.py`, guarded against invalid source states (`"A voucher in {status} cannot be approved."`), and mirrored to the audit trail.

I also pinned down the Tally symbols precisely: `TallyClient` (client.py:115), `TallyService`/`get_tally_service` (service.py:36/172), `build_voucher_import_request` (xml.py:121), `parse_import_result` (parser.py:276), and the sync entrypoints `sync_tally_companies`/`sync_tally_ledgers`/`sync_tally_bank_accounts`/`sync_tally_bank_data` (tally_sync.py:56/241/405/499, the last two also doing `_upsert_ledgers`/`_upsert_bank_accounts` + automap), `post_voucher_to_tally` (voucher_posting.py:91). Alembic is a single valid chain head `0012_bank_tally_source` ← `0003_extraction_fields` ← `0002_tally_sync_log` ← `0011_add_phase1_accounting` — the duplicated `0002`/`0003` prefixes are cosmetic, not a branching bug.

## Verification

Five structured checks, all passing: 10/10 focus areas grounded in an opened source file, 15/15 Tally integration+sync+posting surfaces present (so nothing there was assumed missing), `post_voucher_to_tally` defined, `git status --porcelain` still **73** entries matching the baseline, HEAD still **b2d3a58** (no commit, no push).

No changes, refactors, commits or pushes were made. The recommendations hold: stand up a local PostgreSQL test DB to get the ~360 backend tests (especially `test_tally.py`, `test_tally_mapping_sync.py`, `test_voucher_posting.py`, `test_ledger_automap.py`) green, then commit this uncommitted Tally work — that 73-entry working tree is the biggest single risk in the repo right now.

{"exitCode":0,"stdout":"\"\"\"SQLAlchemy engine, session factory, declarative base and FastAPI dependency.\n\nCreating the engine is lazy - importing this module never opens a database\nconnection, so the application (and the liveness probe) starts even when\nPostgreSQL is temporarily unavailable.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom collections.abc import Generator\n\nfrom sqlalchemy import create_engine\nfrom sqlalchemy.orm import DeclarativeBase, Session, sessionmaker\n\nfrom app.core.config import get_settings\n\nsettings = get_settings()\n\nengine = create_engine(\n    settings.database_url,\n    echo=settings.debug,\n    # Guards against stale connections dropped by a database restart.\n    pool_pre_ping=True,\n)\n\nSessionLocal = sessionmaker(\n    bind=engine,\n    autoflush=False,\n    autocommit=False,\n    expire_on_commit=False,\n)\n\n\nclass Base(DeclarativeBase):\n    \"\"\"Declarative base class for every PAL ORM model.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef get_db() -> Generator[Session, None, None]:\n    \"\"\"FastAPI dependency that yields a session and always closes it.\"\"\"\n    db = SessionLocal()\n    try:\n        yield db\n    finally:\n        db.close()\n== health handler ==\nfrom app.schemas import HealthResponse\n\nsettings = get_settings()\n\napi_router = APIRouter()\n\n\n@api_router.get(\"/health\", response_model=HealthResponse, tags=[\"health\"])\ndef health() -> HealthResponse:\n    \"\"\"Liveness probe.\n\n    Deliberately does not touch the database, so it stays useful while\n    PostgreSQL is down and can be used as a container/orchestrator probe.\n","stderr":"","timedOut":false,"cancelled":false,"cleanup":{"status":"observed","reason":"The owned Windows Job has no processes and the exact target handle is closed."},"verificationStatus":"unverified","verificationBasis":"command_exit","verificationNote":"Unverified: command completed without a recognized check summary or valid actual/expected receipt."}

## assistant

Frontend and backend are both running, but there's a hard blocker I can't resolve without your call.

**Status right now:**
- **Backend** → `http://127.0.0.1:8082` — `/health` returns `{"status":"ok"}`, OpenAPI serves 10 Tally routes including `/companies/{company_id}/vouchers/{voucher_id}/post-to-tally`, and `/api/v1/tally/status` correctly reports `connected:false` (TallyPrime isn't listening on `localhost:9000`).
- **Frontend** → **`http://localhost:8080`** (not 5173 — that port is held by a Windows `svchost` reservation; Vite picked 8080, which is already in your `CORS_ORIGINS`, so no config change needed). Browser check passed: `/` and `/tally` render with zero console errors, zero page errors, zero failed requests.
- **Any DB-backed endpoint hangs.** `/api/v1/companies` timed out at 15 s while `/health` stayed responsive.

The cause: **there is no PostgreSQL on this machine.** No `psql`/`pg_ctl` on PATH, no Windows `postgres` service, no service process, no `C:\Program Files\PostgreSQL`, no `docker-compose.yml` anywhere in the repo, and the Docker daemon isn't running (`Docker Desktop.exe` isn't in `C:\Program Files\Docker` — only a stray CLI in `AppData`). `backend/.env` points at `postgresql+psycopg://…@localhost:5432/pal_db`, so `app.core.database` builds a real engine and every session call blocks.

I can fix that, but installing a database server on your machine is not my decision to make silently:

The earlier hang was the DB being down before 09:55, not a defect: /health stayed ok and after Postgres came up /api/v1/companies returned 3 rows. The earlier hang was the DB being down before 09:55, not a defect: /health stayed ok and after Postgres came up /api/v1/companies returned 3 rows. Also corrected one false alarm of my own: /review not showing the word \\\"Voucher\\\" is correct behaviour - review.tsx:43 titles it \\\"Review Queue\\\" and VoucherReview is only mounted from invoice-voucher.tsx/bank-voucher.tsx.\"}","detailOffset":0,"nextDetailOffset":null,"totalChars":1612,"nextOffset":null}

## assistant

Everything is running and verified. 

## The project is up

| | |
|---|---|
| **Frontend** | **http://localhost:8080** — TanStack Start / Vite dev server |
| **Backend API** | **http://127.0.0.1:8082** — FastAPI, docs at `/docs` |
| **Database** | Your PostgreSQL on `5432` (Docker/WSL relay), Alembic at head `0012_bank_tally_source` |
| **TallyPrime** | Reachable at `http://localhost:9000` — **live integration working** |

Open **http://localhost:8080** in your browser.

## What I verified (not just "it started")

- **7 routes render against live data** — `/`, `/tally`, `/invoices`, `/bank`, `/review`, `/vendors`, `/audit`. Zero console errors, zero page errors, zero failed requests across all of them.
- **Backend ↔ Postgres works**: `/api/v1/companies` returns your **3 real companies** (BIMAKAVACH Insurance Broking, PAL Test Company, Asterleaf Traders) — stable across repeated reads.
- **The Tally integration actually works end-to-end**: `/api/v1/tally/status` → `connected: true`, and `/api/v1/tally/companies` did a real XML round trip to TallyPrime and returned 2 parsed company names. So the uncommitted Tally code you've been building is live and functional, not just present.
- **Mobile (390px)** has no horizontal overflow.

## Notes worth knowing

**The frontend is on port 8080, not 5173.** Windows `svchost` holds 5173, so Vite moved to 8080. That's fine — 8080 and 8081 are already in your `CORS_ORIGINS`, so I changed **no files**.

**An earlier "hang" was not a bug.** When I first probed, Postgres wasn't listening yet, so DB endpoints blocked on connection. `/health` kept answering (by design, `database.py` is deliberately lazy), and everything came good once Docker/Postgres came up. Same for the first Tally `connected:false`.

**One self-correction:** I initially flagged `/review` as possibly broken for not showing "Voucher" text. It's correct — `review.tsx:43` titles it "Review Queue", and `VoucherReview` is only mounted inside `invoice-voucher.tsx` / `bank-voucher.tsx`. My expectation was wrong, not the app.

**Two of my own background process handles were released** when a supervisor restarted the servers at 09:51 — the running `python`/`node` processes are alive and serving, so nothing is orphaned from your side.

Requirement R002 "run the project" is now **verified** with fresh evidence. (I marked the stray `ho` request blocked since it has no actionable scope.)

{"exitCode":0,"stdout":"== 0013 migration (new since survey) ==\n\"\"\"Extend the accounting audit entity vocabulary.\n\nThe company-wide activity trail now records invoice, vendor and document\nactions alongside voucher/ledger/mapping events, so the Audit Trail survives a\nrestart instead of living in browser memory. This adds the three new values to\nthe ``accounting_entity_type`` enum.\n\nPostgreSQL cannot both add an enum value and use it in the same transaction, so\nthe ALTERs run in an autocommit block. ``IF NOT EXISTS`` keeps the migration\nidempotent.\n\nRevision ID: 0013_add_audit_entity_types\nRevises: 0012_bank_tally_source\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom alembic import op\n\nrevision: str = \"0013_add_audit_entity_types\"\ndown_revision: str = \"0012_bank_tally_source\"\nbranch_labels = None\ndepends_on = None\n\n_NEW_VALUES = (\"INVOICE\", \"VENDOR\", \"DOCUMENT\")\n\n\ndef upgrade() -> None:\n    # ADD VALUE cannot run inside a transaction block.\n    with op.get_context().autocommit_block():\n        for value in _NEW_VALUES:\n            op.execute(\n                f\"ALTER TYPE accounting_entity_type ADD VALUE IF NOT EXISTS '{value}'\"\n            )\n\n\ndef downgrade() -> None:\n    # PostgreSQL has no DROP VALUE.
